    10	const EXIT_BY_CLASS = { ok: 0, 'usage-limit': 10, throttle: 11, auth: 12, 'other-error': 13 };

    12	/**
    13	 * `baton detect` — classify harness output / structured error types.
    14	 * Exit codes ARE the verdict (frozen contract): 0 ok · 10 usage-limit ·
    15	 * 11 throttle · 12 auth · 13 other-error · 2 usage error.
    16	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    17	 * @returns {number}
    18	 */
    19	export function cmdDetect(args, io) {
    20	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', text: 'string', 'exit-code': 'string', 'structured-error-type': 'string', signatures: 'string', explain: 'boolean' });
    21	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'detect', parsed.error);
    22	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    23	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    24	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'detect', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    25	
    26	  let table;
    27	  try {
    28	    table = loadSignatures(
    29	      {
    30	        builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES,
    31	        overlayPath: typeof flags.signatures === 'string' ? flags.signatures : undefined,
    32	      },
    33	      io,
    34	    );
    35	  } catch (err) {
    36	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    37	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-signatures', msg } });
    38	    else io.stderr.write(`baton detect: ${msg}\n`);
    39	    return 1;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const text = typeof flags.text === 'string' ? flags.text : typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '';
    43	  const exitCode = flags['exit-code'] !== undefined ? Number(flags['exit-code']) : 0;
    44	
    45	  /** @type {{kind: string, errorType: string} | null} */
    46	  let structured = null;
    47	  if (typeof flags['structured-error-type'] === 'string') {
    48	    structured = {
    49	      kind: platform === 'codex' ? 'exec-json' : 'stop-failure',
    50	      errorType: flags['structured-error-type'],
    51	    };
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  const verdict = classify({ text, exitCode, platform, table, structured });
    55	  const data = { ...verdict, tableVersion: table.schema, tableUpdated: table.updated };
    56	  if (flags.explain) {
    57	    data.tried = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform).map((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id);
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  if (flags.json) {
    61	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    62	  } else {
    63	    io.stdout.write(`${verdict.class}${verdict.signatureId ? ` (${verdict.signatureId}, ${verdict.confidence})` : ''}${verdict.resetHint ? ` — resets ${verdict.resetHint}` : ''}\n`);
    64	  }
    65	  return EXIT_BY_CLASS[verdict.class] ?? 13;
    66	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    54	  const assignments = {};
    55	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    56	    /** @type {any[]} */
    57	    const skipped = [];
    58	    /** @type {any} */
    59	    let assignment = null;
    60	
    61	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    62	      const entry = chain[i];
    63	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    64	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    65	        continue;
    66	      }
    67	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    68	      if (why) {
    69	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    70	        continue;
    71	      }
    72	      assignment = {
    73	        platform: entry.platform,
    74	        model: entry.model,
    75	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    76	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    77	        chainIndex: i,
    78	        skipped,
    79	      };
    80	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    81	      break;
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
    85	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
    86	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
    87	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
    88	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
    89	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    90	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
    91	      }
    92	    }
    93	
    94	    if (!assignment) {
    95	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    96	    }
    97	    assignments[role] = assignment;
    98	  }
    99	
   100	  return { assignments, notes };
   101	}

     1	const PRECEDENCE = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     2	
     3	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
    10	};
    11	
    12	// eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}

     7	//
     8	// Source of truth: docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md
     9	//   §"Model-level failover (Gate-1 iteration 1, finding 4)" and
    10	//   §"Acceptance constraints" constraint 2; docs/design/core.md §Module APIs
    11	//   (resolveRoles). Mirrors the idioms in tests/unit/resolve.test.mjs.
    12	//
    13	// CONTRACT (pinned here):
    14	//   A1. NEW OPT. resolveRoles opts gain `avoidEntries: [{platform, model}]`,
    15	//       alongside the existing platform-level `avoid[]`. A chain entry is
    16	//       skipped by this rule ONLY when BOTH platform AND model match an
    17	//       avoidEntries item — a different model on the same platform stays
    18	//       selectable (that is the whole point: model-level, not platform-level).
    19	//   A2. AUDIT. An entry-avoided skip is recorded in skipped[] with a DISTINCT
    20	//       why: 'avoided-entry' (mirrors the existing skip-recording idiom:
    21	//       {chainIndex, platform, model, why}). The platform-level rule keeps its
    22	//       own why 'avoided' — the two are never conflated.
    23	//   A3. NAMED TEST (constraint 2). A chain whose first entry is codex/<A> and
    24	//       second codex/<B>, with avoidEntries [{platform:'codex', model:'<A>'}],
    25	//       selects <B> on the SAME platform (mode stays native), with no
    26	//       platform-wide avoidance.
    27	//   A4. COMPOSITION. avoidEntries composes with avoid[]: an entry on an AVOIDED
    28	//       PLATFORM is skipped by the platform rule (why 'avoided'); entry
    29	//       avoidance never resurrects a platform-avoided entry.
    30	//   A5. EFFORT SUFFIX. Config chain strings are "platform/model[@effort]"
    31	//       (matrix.mjs parses model + effort apart). avoidEntries matches on MODEL
    32	//       IDENTITY regardless of any @effort suffix — model is what's

    35	//       differences never prevent (or force) a match.
    36	//   A6. EXHAUSTION. All entries avoided → the existing 'unavailable' path fires
    37	//       (platform/model null, full skipped[]), never a crash.
    38	//   A7. BACKWARD COMPAT. Omitted OR empty avoidEntries → behavior identical to

    60	//      Precedence when multiple signatures match: usage-limit > auth > throttle

    65	//      match[1] ?? match[0] (first capture group if present, else whole match);
    66	//      no regex or no match -> resetHint null. A matched signature with no
    67	//      resetHint -> resetHint null.
    68	//   4. EXIT-CODE RULE. A matching signature WINS regardless of exit code. With
    69	//      NO matching signature: nonzero exit -> other-error (NEVER usage-limit);
    70	//      zero exit -> ok. No-match verdicts carry signatureId null, resetHint null.
    71	//   5. json-field matcher parses each LINE of text as JSON and compares a dotted
    72	//      path to `equals`.

    76	const ANSI_RESET = '\x1b[0m';

    10	// ===========================================================================
    11	// Contract choices for detect/signatures.mjs + core/data/signatures.v1.json.
    12	// Source of truth: docs/design/core.md §Module APIs (loadSignatures signature,
    13	// "validates, lints regexes"), §"signatures/classifier" test list; plan
    14	// §Core engine — Limit detector; docs/design/platform-notes.md (verified
    15	// limit strings). Readings this file PINS where the spec left room:
    16	//
    17	//   A. TABLE SHAPE (loadSignatures return value): a flat object
    18	//        { schema: 'baton/signatures@1', updated: <ISO-ish date string>,
    19	//          signatures: Signature[] }
    20	//      Signatures are a FLAT array (not grouped by platform); each carries its
    21	//      own `platform`. The classifier filters by that field. Override-by-id and
    22	//      the classifier both operate over `table.signatures`.
    23	//
    24	//   B. SIGNATURE SHAPE:
    25	//        { id: string,            // unique; overlay merge key
    26	//          platform: 'claude-code'|'codex'|'cursor',
    27	//          class: 'usage-limit'|'auth'|'throttle'|'other-error',
    28	//          confidence: 'high'|'medium'|'low',
    29	//          matcher: Matcher,
    30	//          resetHint?: string }   // OPTIONAL regex (string) used by the
    31	//                                 // classifier to extract a reset hint.
    32	//      Matcher kinds (the only three accepted):
    33	//        { kind:'substring', value:string }
    34	//        { kind:'regex', pattern:string, flags?:string }
    35	//        { kind:'json-field', path:string /* dotted */, equals:<value> }
    36	//
    37	//   C. VALIDATION FAILURES THROW. loadSignatures returns a Table on success;
    38	//      an invalid built-in or overlay table (bad matcher kind, over-long
    39	//      regex, syntactically-invalid regex, backreference, pathological nested
    40	//      quantifier, unknown platform key) throws an Error. Error messages are
    41	//      asserted only with permissive keyword regexes.
    42	//      - regex pattern length cap: 200 chars (201 rejected).
    43	//      - a syntactically INVALID regex is rejected with a PATHED error — the
    44	//        message names the offending signature id (fold C1-3).
    45	//      - backreferences (\1) rejected (PIN: the open lookbehind question is
    46	//        resolved to "backreferences rejected"; lookbehind is left UNPINNED so
    47	//        the implementer keeps latitude there).
    48	//      - a pathological nested-quantifier construct like (a+)+ is rejected as a
    49	//        non-linear-time / catastrophic-backtracking risk (fold C1-3). classify()
    50	//        stays SYNCHRONOUS (no options.timeBudgetMs); the runtime time-guard is
    51	//        achieved BY CONSTRUCTION — because the lint forbids such patterns from
    52	//        ever shipping or being overlaid, classify() cannot hang on adversarial
    53	//        input. The same lints apply to the OVERLAY path.
    54	//      - unknown platform key (not one of the three) rejected.
    55	//
    56	//   D. OVERLAY (overlayPath): merged over the built-in by signature `id`,
    57	//      LATER WINS — an overlay id equal to a built-in id replaces it; a new
    58	//      overlay id is appended. A missing/undefined overlayPath = no overlay.
    59	//
    60	//   E. BUILT-IN PIN: the real shipped file core/data/signatures.v1.json is
    61	//      loaded through the loader via node:fs (a minimal real-fs io = {fs}) so
    62	//      the shipped data — the verified per-platform strings from
    63	//      platform-notes.md — is pinned, not just the loader's mechanics.

    73	// Apply a matcher the same way the classifier is contracted to, so the built-in
    74	// content pins are robust to a substring-vs-regex authoring choice for the
    75	// flexible cases (e.g. the 429 other-error entry).
    76	function matcherMatches(matcher, text) {
    77	  if (!matcher) return false;
    78	  if (matcher.kind === 'substring') return text.includes(matcher.value);
    79	  if (matcher.kind === 'regex') return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    80	  if (matcher.kind === 'json-field') {
    81	    for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    82	      let obj;
    83	      try {
    84	        obj = JSON.parse(line);
    85	      } catch {
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      const val = String(matcher.path).split('.').reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    89	      if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    90	    }
    91	    return false;
    92	  }
    93	  return false;
    94	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { probeRegexSafe } from './probe.mjs';
     2	
     3	const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
     4	const CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     5	const MATCHER_KINDS = ['substring', 'regex', 'json-field'];
     6	const REGEX_MAX_LEN = 200;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * Is a quantified group's body ambiguous? Alternation, another quantifier, or
    10	 * any nested group inside a `(…)+`/`(…)*`/`(…){` makes backtracking
    11	 * super-linear in the worst case — conservatively rejected (gate-2 fix 6:
    12	 * the old lint accepted `(a|aa)+$` and the `(a|a)*x` live repro).
    13	 * @param {string} body
    14	 */
    15	function riskyGroupBody(body) {
    16	  // Strip a non-capture/lookaround/named marker so `(?:abc)+` reads as `abc`.
    17	  const b = body.replace(/^\?(?:<=|<!|:|=|!|<[^>]*>)/, '');
    18	  let inClass = false;
    19	  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
    20	    const c = b[i];
    21	    if (c === '\\') {
    22	      i++;
    23	      continue;
    24	    }
    25	    if (inClass) {
    26	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    27	      continue;
    28	    }
    29	    if (c === '[') {
    30	      inClass = true;
    31	      continue;
    32	    }
    33	    if (c === '|' || c === '+' || c === '*' || c === '{' || c === '?' || c === '(' || c === ')') return true;
    34	  }
    35	  return false;
    36	}
    37	
    38	/**
    39	 * Structural scan: walk the pattern (class- and escape-aware), and for every
    40	 * group followed by an unbounded quantifier check its full body — any nesting
    41	 * depth — for ambiguity. Returns a problem string or null.
    42	 * @param {string} pattern
    43	 */
    44	function scanQuantifiedGroups(pattern) {
    45	  /** @type {number[]} */
    46	  const opens = [];
    47	  let inClass = false;
    48	  for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
    49	    const c = pattern[i];
    50	    if (c === '\\') {
    51	      i++;
    52	      continue;
    53	    }
    54	    if (inClass) {
    55	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    56	      continue;
    57	    }
    58	    if (c === '[') {
    59	      inClass = true;
    60	      continue;
    61	    }
    62	    if (c === '(') {
    63	      opens.push(i);
    64	      continue;
    65	    }
    66	    if (c === ')') {
    67	      const start = opens.pop();
    68	      if (start === undefined) continue; // unbalanced — new RegExp() reports it
    69	      const next = pattern[i + 1];
    70	      if (next === '+' || next === '*' || next === '{') {
    71	        const body = pattern.slice(start + 1, i);
    72	        if (riskyGroupBody(body)) {
    73	          return `quantified group "(${body})" contains alternation, another quantifier, or a nested group`;
    74	        }
    75	      }
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	  return null;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Approximate character-set overlap between two simple-atom alphabets.
    83	 * Descriptors: {all:true} (dot / broad negated shorthand) | {sh:'d'|'w'|'s'} |
    84	 * {lit:Set<string>}. Returns true unless the two sets are PROVABLY disjoint —
    85	 * conservative for untrusted overlays (iter-4 I4).
    86	 * @param {any} a @param {any} b
    87	 */
    88	function alphasOverlap(a, b) {
    89	  if (!a || !b) return false;
    90	  if (a.all || b.all) return true;
    91	  if (a.sh && b.sh) {
    92	    if (a.sh === b.sh) return true;
    93	    // \s is disjoint from \d and \w; \d ⊂ \w so they overlap.
    94	    return !(a.sh === 's' || b.sh === 's');
    95	  }
    96	  if (a.lit && b.lit) {
    97	    for (const c of a.lit) if (b.lit.has(c)) return true;
    98	    return false;
    99	  }
   100	  const sh = a.sh ? a.sh : b.sh;
   101	  const lit = a.lit ? a.lit : b.lit;
   102	  for (const c of lit) {
   103	    if (sh === 'd' && c >= '0' && c <= '9') return true;
   104	    if (sh === 'w' && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(c)) return true;
   105	    if (sh === 's' && /\s/.test(c)) return true;
   106	  }
   107	  return false;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/**
   111	 * Structural scan for a run of ≥2 CONSECUTIVE atoms, each with an UNBOUNDED
   112	 * quantifier (`*`, `+`, `{n,}`), whose alphabets overlap — `.*.*…`, `\d*\d*…`,
   113	 * `\(*\(*…`, `\n*\n*…`, `[a-z]*[a-z]*…`. Such a chain partitions the same input
   114	 * combinatorially and backtracks super-linearly, yet is invisible to the
   115	 * quantified-GROUP scan; and because a finite probe alphabet cannot guarantee a
   116	 * non-matching tail for every atom (`.` accepts almost everything), rejecting
   117	 * the STRUCTURE at load is the reliable guard (iter-4 I4, reviewer-a #7 /
   118	 * reviewer-b #3). Groups reset the run (the group scan covers quantified groups;
   119	 * a group's overlap with a simple atom is not decided here). Returns a problem
   120	 * string or null.
   121	 * @param {string} pattern
   122	 * @returns {string | null}
   123	 */
   124	function scanChainedQuantifiedAtoms(pattern) {
   125	  const p = pattern;
   126	  /** @type {any} */
   127	  let prev = null; // alpha of the previous atom IF it carried an unbounded quantifier, else null
   128	
   129	  for (let i = 0; i < p.length; ) {
   130	    const c = p[i];
   131	    // Chain-breakers: alternation and anchors end any run.
   132	    if (c === '|' || c === '^' || c === '$') {
   133	      prev = null;
   134	      i += 1;
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    /** @type {any} */
   138	    let alpha = null;
   139	    let isGroup = false;
   140	    if (c === '\\') {
   141	      const n = p[i + 1];
   142	      // decode length of the escape token
   143	      let len = 2;
   144	      if (n === 'x') len = 4;
   145	      else if (n === 'u') len = p[i + 2] === '{' ? (p.indexOf('}', i) - i + 1) : 6;
   146	      if (n === 'd' || n === 'w' || n === 's') alpha = { sh: n };
   147	      else if (n === 'D' || n === 'W' || n === 'S' || n === 'b' || n === 'B') alpha = { all: true }; // broad/negated or anchor-ish → conservative
   148	      else alpha = { lit: new Set([decodeEscape(p.slice(i, i + len))]) }; // \(, \n, \t, \xNN, \uNNNN → the literal char
   149	      i += Math.max(2, len);
   150	    } else if (c === '[') {
   151	      const end = classEnd(p, i);
   152	      const body = p.slice(i + 1, end);
   153	      alpha = body.startsWith('^') ? { all: true } : classAlpha(body);
   154	      i = end + 1;
   155	    } else if (c === '(') {
   156	      // Skip the whole group (balance-aware); recurse into its body for chains.
   157	      const end = groupEnd(p, i);
   158	      const inner = scanChainedQuantifiedAtoms(p.slice(i + 1, end));
   159	      if (inner) return inner;
   160	      isGroup = true;
   161	      i = end + 1;
   162	    } else if (c === '.') {
   163	      alpha = { all: true };
   164	      i += 1;
   165	    } else {
   166	      alpha = { lit: new Set([c]) };
   167	      i += 1;
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    // Read an optional quantifier and classify it as unbounded or not.
   171	    let unbounded = false;
   172	    const q = p[i];
   173	    if (q === '*' || q === '+') {
   174	      unbounded = true;
   175	      i += 1;
   176	    } else if (q === '{') {
   177	      const close = p.indexOf('}', i);
   178	      if (close !== -1) {
   179	        const spec = p.slice(i + 1, close);
   180	        // {n,} (no upper bound) is unbounded; {n} and {n,m} are bounded.
   181	        unbounded = /^\d+,\s*$/.test(spec);
   182	        i = close + 1;
   183	      }
   184	    } else if (q === '?') {
   185	      i += 1;
   186	    }
   187	    // consume a lazy/possessive modifier if present
   188	    if (p[i] === '?' || p[i] === '+') i += 1;
   189	
   190	    if (isGroup) {
   191	      // A group breaks the simple-atom run.
   192	      prev = null;
   193	      continue;
   194	    }
   195	    // prev + current = a run of ≥2 CONSECUTIVE overlapping unbounded-quantified
   196	    // atoms → super-linear backtracking. Reject.
   197	    if (unbounded && prev && alphasOverlap(prev, alpha)) {
   198	      return 'chained overlapping unbounded quantifiers (e.g. `.*.*`, `\\d*\\d*`, `\\(*\\(*`) backtrack super-linearly';
   199	    }
   200	    prev = unbounded ? alpha : null;
   201	  }
   202	  return null;
   203	}
   204	
   205	/** Decode a single-char escape token (`\(`, `\n`, `\t`, `\xNN`, `\uNNNN`, `\u{...}`) to its literal char. @param {string} tok */
   206	function decodeEscape(tok) {
   207	  const rest = tok.slice(1);
   208	  if (rest[0] === 'x') return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(rest.slice(1, 3), 16));
   209	  if (rest[0] === 'u') {
   210	    const h = rest[1] === '{' ? rest.slice(2, rest.indexOf('}')) : rest.slice(1, 5);
   211	    try {
   212	      return String.fromCodePoint(parseInt(h, 16));
   213	    } catch {
   214	      return rest[0];
   215	    }
   216	  }
   217	  const named = { n: '\n', t: '\t', r: '\r', f: '\f', v: '\v', 0: '\0' };
   218	  return named[/** @type {'n'} */ (rest[0])] ?? rest[0];
   219	}
   220	
   221	/** Index of the `]` closing a class opened at `open` (a `]` immediately after `[`/`[^` is a literal). @param {string} p @param {number} open */
   222	function classEnd(p, open) {
   223	  let i = open + 1;
   224	  if (p[i] === '^') i += 1;
   225	  if (p[i] === ']') i += 1; // leading ] is literal
   226	  for (; i < p.length; i++) {
   227	    if (p[i] === '\\') {
   228	      i += 1;
   229	      continue;
   230	    }
   231	    if (p[i] === ']') return i;
   232	  }
   233	  return p.length - 1;
   234	}
   235	
   236	/** Index of the `)` closing a group opened at `open` (balance-aware, class/escape-safe). @param {string} p @param {number} open */
   237	function groupEnd(p, open) {
   238	  let depth = 0;
   239	  let inClass = false;
   240	  for (let i = open; i < p.length; i++) {
   241	    const c = p[i];
   242	    if (c === '\\') {
   243	      i += 1;
   244	      continue;
   245	    }
   246	    if (inClass) {
   247	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
   248	      continue;
   249	    }
   250	    if (c === '[') inClass = true;
   251	    else if (c === '(') depth += 1;
   252	    else if (c === ')') {
   253	      depth -= 1;
   254	      if (depth === 0) return i;
   255	    }
   256	  }
   257	  return p.length - 1;
   258	}
   259	
   260	/** Approximate alphabet of a (non-negated) class body: literals + range endpoints + shorthands. @param {string} body */

     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { resolveRoles } from '../../core/src/roles/resolve.mjs';
     4	
     5	// ===========================================================================
     6	// Contract choices for roles/resolve.mjs. Source of truth: docs/design/core.md
     7	// §Module APIs (resolveRoles signature + assignment shape); plan §Core engine —
     8	// Role matrix + Availability probing (capability/outcome dimensions, avoid[],
     9	// native/delegated/forced-default/unavailable, chainIndex + skipped audit data).
    10	// resolveRoles is PURE and explainable.
    11	//
    12	// INPUT: resolveRoles({config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null}).
    13	//   `config` is the PARSED config (roles as arrays of {platform, model, effort},
    14	//   as loadConfig returns) — built inline here so this file's red phase is
    15	//   attributable to resolve.mjs alone (not matrix.mjs).
    16	//
    17	// RETURN: { assignments: {[role]: Assignment}, notes: [] }.
    18	//   Assignment = {platform, model, effort, mode, chainIndex, skipped[], degraded?}.
    19	//   modes: 'native' | 'delegated' | 'forced-default' | 'unavailable'.
    20	//
    21	// PINS:
    22	//   1. CHAIN WALK. For each role, walk entries in order; the FIRST eligible
    23	//      (surviving) entry is selected. skipped[] records each passed-over entry
    24	//      as {chainIndex, platform, model, why} (extra fields tolerated).
    25	//      why values: 'avoided' | 'unknown-platform' | 'disabled' | 'rate-limited' | 'unauthenticated'.
    26	//   2. ELIGIBILITY / skip reasons:
    27	//        - platform ∈ avoid                          -> skip, why 'avoided'
    28	//        - platform NOT a key of config.platforms    -> skip, why 'unknown-platform'
    29	//        - config.platforms[p].enabled === false     -> skip, why 'disabled'
    30	//        - probes given & probes[p].outcome==='rate-limited' -> skip, why 'rate-limited'
    31	//        - probes given & capability==='installed' AND outcome!=='ok' (a VERIFIED
    32	//          auth/reachability FAILURE)                 -> skip, why 'unauthenticated'
    33	//          (iter-3 F1: a capped SUCCESS {installed, ok} leaves auth UNVERIFIED —
    34	//          it stays selectable and is flagged degraded, it is NOT skipped)
    35	//      A platform with capability ∈ {authenticated, reachable} AND outcome
    36	//      ≠ 'rate-limited' is eligible.
    37	//   3. DEGRADED. If probes is a non-null object but has NO entry for the chosen
    38	//      platform (unverifiable), the entry is selectable with degraded === true.
    39	//      probes === null means OFFLINE resolution: still selectable, but every
    40	//      selection is flagged degraded (gate-2 iter-2 M3; plan: offline always degraded).
    41	//      Non-degraded assignments have a falsy `degraded` (absent or false).
    42	//   4. MODE. First surviving entry: mode 'native' iff entry.platform === to,
    43	//      else 'delegated'. chainIndex = that entry's index in the ORIGINAL chain.
    44	//   5. nativeOnly restricts consideration to entries with platform === to; if
    45	//      none survive it MAY fall back to config.defaults[to] with mode
    46	//      'forced-default' (platform=to, model=defaults[to], effort=null,
    47	//      chainIndex=null) — but ONLY when the destination `to` is itself
    48	//      probe-eligible (PIN 2). When `to` is probe-INELIGIBLE (e.g. rate-limited),
    49	//      the forced-default is SUPPRESSED and the role resolves 'unavailable', the
    50	//      ineligibility audited in skipped[] (why 'rate-limited'). (fold C1-1)
    51	//   6. UNAVAILABLE. In normal (non-nativeOnly) mode, when NOTHING survives:
    52	//      mode 'unavailable', platform null, model null, effort null,
    53	//      chainIndex null, and skipped[] carries the full passed-over list.
    54	//   7. DETERMINISM. Same input -> deep-equal output; input config is not mutated.
    55	//      assignments object keys are in the CONFIG'S ROLE ORDER.
    56	//   8. avoid[] auto-population is the CALLER'S job; resolve only honors the list.
    57	// ===========================================================================
    58	
    59	const e = (platform, model, effort = null) => ({ platform, model, effort });
    60	
    61	const PLATFORMS = { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} };
    62	const DEFAULTS = { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' };
    63	
    64	// The full seven-role config in parsed form (mirrors the real baton.config.json).
    65	function fullConfig() {
    66	  return {
    67	    schema: 'baton/config@1',
    68	    roles: {
    69	      planner: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5')],
    70	      'plan-reviewer': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh')],
    71	      'test-author': [e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
    72	      'test-verifier': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
    73	      implementer: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('cursor', 'composer')],
    74	      'final-reviewer-a': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
    75	      'final-reviewer-b': [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
    76	    },
    77	    platforms: { ...PLATFORMS },
    78	    defaults: { ...DEFAULTS },
    79	  };
    80	}

     1	import { loadConfig } from '../roles/matrix.mjs';
     2	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
     3	import { readProbeCache } from '../roles/availability.mjs';
     4	import { emitEnvelope, resolveRoot, usageError, parseFlagsStrict, platformError } from './shared.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * `baton remap` — resolve the committed role matrix for a destination platform.
     8	 * Read-only: prints assignments, never mutates the bundle (receive owns that).
     9	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    10	 * @returns {number}
    11	 */
    12	export function cmdRemap(args, io) {
    13	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { to: 'string', avoid: 'string', 'native-only': 'boolean' });
    14	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'remap', parsed.error);
    15	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    16	  const to = typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null;
    17	  if (!to) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', '--to <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    18	  const pErr = platformError(to);
    19	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', pErr);
    20	
    21	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    22	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
    23	  if (!config) {
    24	    const msg = errors.map((e) => (e.path ? `${e.path}: ${e.msg}` : e.msg)).join('; ');
    25	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-config', msg } });
    26	    else io.stderr.write(`baton remap: ${msg}\n`);
    27	    return 1;
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  const avoid = typeof flags.avoid === 'string' ? flags.avoid.split(',') : [];
    31	  const { assignments, notes } = resolveRoles({
    32	    config,
    33	    to,
    34	    avoid,
    35	    nativeOnly: flags['native-only'] === true,
    36	    // The fresh doctor probe cache feeds eligibility (gate-2 major 13):
    37	    // rate-limited platforms are skipped like avoid[] entries; a stale or
    38	    // absent cache resolves offline with degraded flags.
    39	    probes: readProbeCache(root, io),
    40	  });
    41	
    42	  if (flags.json) {
    43	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { assignments, notes } });
    44	  } else {
    45	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(assignments)) {
    46	      io.stdout.write(`${role}: ${a.platform ?? '—'}/${a.model ?? '—'}${a.effort ? `@${a.effort}` : ''} (${a.mode})\n`);
    47	    }
    48	  }
    49	  return 0;
    50	}

+    const r = classify({ text, exitCode: 1, platform: 'codex', table: PREC });
+    assert.equal(r.class, 'usage-limit', 'a real usage limit outranks a model-unavailable rejection');

   329	function lintSignature(s) {
   330	  if (typeof s.id !== 'string' || s.id.length === 0) throw new Error('signature with missing id');
   331	  if (!PLATFORMS.includes(s.platform)) {
   332	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown platform "${s.platform}" (expected ${PLATFORMS.join('|')})`);
   333	  }
   334	  if (!CLASSES.includes(s.class)) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown class "${s.class}"`);
   335	  if (!s.matcher || !MATCHER_KINDS.includes(s.matcher.kind)) {
   336	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown matcher kind "${s.matcher?.kind}" (expected ${MATCHER_KINDS.join('|')})`);
   337	  }
   338	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'regex') lintRegex(s.id, s.matcher.pattern, s.matcher.flags ?? '');
   339	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'substring' && typeof s.matcher.value !== 'string') {
   340	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: substring matcher needs a string value`);
   341	  }
   342	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'json-field' && typeof s.matcher.path !== 'string') {
   343	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: json-field matcher needs a dotted string path`);
   344	  }
   345	  if (s.resetHint !== undefined) lintRegex(s.id, s.resetHint);
   346	}

   349	function readTable(fs, path, opts = {}) {

   351	  const table = JSON.parse(raw);
   352	  if (table?.schema !== 'baton/signatures@1') {
   353	    throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: expected schema baton/signatures@1, got ${JSON.stringify(table?.schema)}`);
   354	  }
   355	  if (!Array.isArray(table.signatures)) throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: signatures must be an array`);
